# Sprint Plan — On-Chain Fraud & MEV Detector v2

**Mode:** Solo developer · 2-week sprints · MVP-first
**MVP target (Definition of Done):** Roadmap Phase 5 — *the first demonstrable intelligence platform*. A live block → detection → simulation → incident → risk score → API/WebSocket path, fully event-sourced and auditable.
**Source of truth:** [onchain_mev_detector_v2_microservices_rev2.md](../../onchain_mev_detector_v2_microservices_rev2.md) — §§ references below point into it.
**Path to GA:** see [production_readiness.md](../../production_readiness.md) — the MVP sprints below must bake in the "Bucket 1 / shift-left" non-negotiables (idempotency, reorg rollback, revm sandbox, audit completeness) from day one; production hardening runs as gated epics after.

> **Sequencing principle.** The architecture is event-driven, so the *event schema and event store come first* (§22: "domain event schema locked"). Every later sprint is a producer/consumer on a backbone that already exists. Phases 1–4 are "the unglamorous foundation — doing them right is the senior signal" (§22). Don't skip the foundation to demo the API.



---

## Velocity & scope assumptions (solo)

- ~1 deployable service or major subsystem reaches "vertical slice working" per sprint; large services (detection, intelligence) span two sprints.
- Each sprint ends with: green CI (clippy + fmt + test + bench-smoke, §20), a runnable demo of the slice, and updated docs.
- Infra (Kafka, RabbitMQ, ClickHouse, Postgres, Redis) runs locally via `docker-compose` from Sprint 0 — no cloud K8s until post-MVP (Phase 10).
- "Provisional/finality" semantics are baked in from the first event-emitting sprint, not retrofitted (§15).

**MVP = Sprints 0–8 (≈18 weeks).** Phases 6–13 are mapped as a post-MVP backlog at the end (Phases 11–13 = the strategic extensions: trader-protection product, predictive liquidation-cascade engine, cross-chain MEV).

---

## Datastore roles (why each store exists)

Stores are split by **access pattern**, not by service. Don't reach for the wrong one when a sprint adds state (§14, line 678–686).

| Store | Type | Holds | Why it, not the others |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Kafka** | Event transport | Domain events in flight (facts), one topic per event type, partitioned by chain | A log for fan-out + deterministic replay (backtesting). Bounded retention — it's the wire, not the record (§2, §4). |
| **ClickHouse** | OLAP / columnar | Append-only **immutable** firehoses: the event store (system of record), incident analytics projection, entity-graph adjacency, usage events | Event-sourced data never updates/deletes — `MergeTree` is built for that. Queries are wide scans + aggregations over huge row counts, and it stays queryable by business key (`address`, `incident_id`, `block_hash`) and retained forever — which Kafka can't be (§4, line 149/153/383/678). |
| **Postgres** | OLTP / relational | **Mutable, transactional** state: in-flight simulation jobs, confirmed incidents, labels/entities with provenance, accounts | Row-oriented, transactional, updated in place — the opposite of ClickHouse's append-only model (line 280, 681–686). |
| **Redis** | Cache | Hot reads + risk-score cache keyed by `(address, model_version)`, invalidated on input change | Sub-ms lookups for the API hot path; never the source of truth (§8.3). |
| **RabbitMQ** | Work queue | The one **command** — `SimulationJob` — competing-consumer, per-job ack/redelivery, DLX, priority | A work queue, not a log; commands consumed once and never entering the event store (§7). |

**Rule of thumb:** *facts in flight → Kafka; immutable facts at rest → ClickHouse; mutable records → Postgres; hot cache → Redis; one-shot work → RabbitMQ.* No cross-service DB joins — services share data via events or read APIs (line 688).

### Sprint 1 — Event backbone & event store (Week 2-3) — DONE

**Goal:** The audit backbone exists and is the system of record (§4, Principle 2).

| # | Task | Est | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Lock the domain event schema** (§2 — all event families, versioned) — the contract every other sprint depends on | 8h | DONE — wire-format golden lock ([crates/events/tests/wire_format.rs](../../crates/events/tests/wire_format.rs)) pins every variant byte-for-byte + exhaustiveness guard (`strum::EnumCount`/`VariantNames`); versioning policy in [crates/events/SCHEMA.md](../../crates/events/SCHEMA.md) |
| 2 | event-store-service: append API (write-authenticated, internal), ClickHouse `MergeTree` append-only storage partitioned by `(chain, event_type, date)` (§4, §14) | 10h | DONE — [crates/event-store/](../../crates/event-store/): bearer-authenticated `POST /v1/events` + Kafka consumer both feed one append-only ClickHouse `MergeTree` partitioned by `(chain, event_type, toDate(occurred_at))`; versioned up/down migrations ([migrations/](../../crates/event-store/migrations/), `just ch-migrate-*`); Swagger UI at `/swagger-ui`. Production-hardened (typed errors, `SecretString` secrets, graceful shutdown, tower-http timeout/body-limit/trace). Verified live against ClickHouse+Kafka ([tests/integration.rs](../../crates/event-store/tests/integration.rs)) |
| 3 | Query API: by address / incident / time range; replay stream by event type + window (§4, §18) | 6h | DONE — [crates/event-store/src/query.rs](../../crates/event-store/src/query.rs): `GET /v1/audit/incident/{id}`, `/v1/address/{addr}/events`, `/v1/replay` (event-type + half-open time window). Business keys (`incident_id`, `addresses`) denormalized into indexed columns at write via `DomainEvent::incident_id()`/`addresses()` (exhaustive, no `_` arm) with bloom-filter skip indexes ([migration 0002](../../crates/event-store/migrations/0002_business_key_columns.up.sql)); deterministic `(occurred_at, event_id)` ordering + keyset pagination (cursor, no silent truncation); unbounded replay refused (400). Internal read surface (unauthenticated, fronted by §11). Verified live against ClickHouse ([tests/integration.rs](../../crates/event-store/tests/integration.rs)) |
| 4 | Kafka topic-per-event-type, partitioned by chain (§20) | 4h | DONE — topic-naming contract (`mev.events.<EventType>`) locked in [crates/events](../../crates/events/src/lib.rs) (`TOPIC_PREFIX`/`topic_for`/`all_topics`, driven by the strum `VARIANTS` set so it can't drift from the schema); the event-store provisions one topic per event type up front — idempotent `kafka::ensure_topics` ([crates/event-store/src/kafka.rs](../../crates/event-store/src/kafka.rs)) via the rdkafka `AdminClient`, run on boot and as the `event-store provision-topics` subcommand, with partition/replication from config (`KAFKA_TOPIC_PARTITIONS`/`KAFKA_TOPIC_REPLICATION`) — so the topology is explicit, not reliant on broker auto-create. Chain is the message key (per-chain ordering on one partition). Consumer subscribes to all topics via regex. Verified live against Kafka ([tests/integration.rs](../../crates/event-store/tests/integration.rs)). Producer publishing lands with ingestion in Sprint 2 |

**Deliverable:** Any event published to Kafka lands immutably in the event store and is queryable by business key. `GET /audit/...` returns an ordered event sequence (§4 audit use case).
**Status: met (live-verified).** All 4 Docker integration tests green against real ClickHouse + Kafka (`cargo test -p event-store --test integration -- --ignored`): append round-trips every event family, Kafka-published events land in the store, query-by-incident/address/window works, and topic-per-event-type provisioning is correct.

### Sprint 2 — Ingestion + reorg-safe assembler (Week 4-5) — DONE

**Goal:** Real chain data flows into the backbone, reorg-aware.

| # | Task | Est | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Source adapter: RPC failover pool (health-checked, circuit-broken) — adapter #3; reth ExEx deferred to Phase 8 (§5) | 8h | DONE — [crates/ingestion/](../../crates/ingestion/): `RpcFailoverPool` ([source/rpc.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/source/rpc.rs)) tries endpoints in preference order over alloy providers, with a per-endpoint 3-state `CircuitBreaker` ([source/circuit.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/source/circuit.rs)), per-call timeout (a hang counts as failure), and an active `eth_chainId` health probe that quarantines wrong-chain endpoints. Sits behind the `ChainSource` trait (reth-ExEx/node-IPC adapters #1/#2 slot in later) and feeds an ordered head poller ([source/head_stream.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/source/head_stream.rs)). Failover + breaker-trip + quarantine verified hermetically against in-process mock RPC servers ([tests/failover.rs](../../crates/ingestion/tests/failover.rs)) |
| 2 | In-memory reorg-aware block tree, bounded by finalization depth (§5, §14) | 8h | DONE — [crates/ingestion/src/tree.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/tree.rs): pure, synchronous `BlockTree` — a `parent_hash`-linked DAG of observed heads with one canonical path. Fork choice is greatest `(number, insertion-seq)` (no post-merge difficulty/attestations on a header-only feed), so a higher block or a newer same-height block wins while back-filled ancestors never displace the tip. `add_block` returns an `#[must_use] AddOutcome` (`Canonical`/`Fork`/`Duplicate`/`MissingParent`) — the seam tasks 3–4 emit events against; the reorg "walk to common ancestor" is computed once here, yielding `CanonicalUpdate { reverted (tip-first rollback order), canonicalized (ancestor-first apply order) }`. Bounded by finalization depth via a pure prune backstop, independent of the authoritative `finalize` path; reorgs at/below the finalized floor are refused (`ReorgBeyondFinality`). 21 unit tests over hand-built forks (no mock RPC). Event emission + the async back-fill driver land in tasks 3–4 |
| 3 | Emit `RawBlockReceived`, `BlockAssembled`, `BlockCanonicalized`, `BlockReverted`, `BlockFinalized` (§5) | 4h | DONE — [crates/ingestion/src/pipeline.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/pipeline.rs): the `Pipeline` owns the block tree (single writer) and maps its outcomes onto events through an `EventSink` seam. The outcome→events decision is **pure** (free fns `observed_events`/`canonical_events`, unit-tested with `assert_eq!`, no runtime), the `Pipeline` is the thin effectful shell. A first-seen head emits `RawBlockReceived` + `BlockAssembled` (`tx_count` carried free from the header fetch; `trace_available` is a source-adapter property — `false` for the header-only RPC pool); the `finalize` tick emits `BlockFinalized` ascending. Published via [publisher.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/publisher.rs) — the system's **first event producer** (rdkafka `FutureProducer`), routed to the schema-derived topic, keyed by chain (per-chain order on one partition, §20), with W3C trace headers injected so the event-store consumer continues the trace (§19). At-least-once: a transient publish failure is retried (cancellable backoff) over a stable-`event_id` envelope so a broker blip can't leave a hole in the audit stream; a permanent (encode) failure is logged + skipped — mirrors the event-store consumer's transient/permanent split. Typed transport-agnostic `PublishError` (no rdkafka leak through the seam) |
| 4 | Reorg handling: walk to common ancestor, emit reverted + canonical events (§5, §15) | 6h | DONE — same `Pipeline::ingest` ([pipeline.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/pipeline.rs)): the **source-driven back-fill walk** over the tree's `MissingParent` seam — an iterative stack fetches absent ancestors via `ChainSource::head_by_hash` bottom-up (a back-fill *fetch* failure self-heals: the next poll re-drives the walk and already-published blocks return `Duplicate`). When the competing branch reaches the tip's height the tree yields the whole swap; `canonical_events` emits `BlockReverted` tip-first (rollback order), each paired by height to its replacement for `replaced_by`, then `BlockCanonicalized` ancestor-first (apply order) — revert-before-apply per §15. The loop lives in the lib as `Pipeline::run` (symmetric with `run_head_poller`), wired in [main.rs](../../crates/ingestion/src/main.rs) as a single-writer task selecting over the head channel + a finality ticker. 12 pipeline tests (pure mapping, duplicate suppression, back-fill ordering, full reorg swap, incremental finalize, publish retry + shutdown-aborts-retry) |

**Deliverable:** Live mainnet blocks produce a correct event stream; a forced reorg (replayed historical fork) emits `BlockReverted` for orphans and re-canonicalizes. **Phase 1 complete.**
**Status:** code complete — all four tasks implemented, 32 unit + 3 hermetic failover tests green, clippy/fmt clean. Live-mainnet run and a replayed-fork reorg check are the remaining verification step before closing the sprint.

### Sprint 3 — Detection service core + first detectors (Week 6-7) — DONE

**Goal:** Fast path emits provisional alerts in < 1s, attribution-blind (§6).

| # | Task | Est | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `DetectorPlugin` trait + compile-time plugin registration (§6) | 5h | DONE — [crates/detection/](../../crates/detection/): the [`DetectorPlugin`](../../crates/detection/src/plugin.rs) seam (`id`/`version`/`kind`/`scope`/`detect`) every detector crate implements, attribution-blind by construction (handed a `DetectionCtx` carrying **no labels**, returns behaviour-only `Evidence` — names the pattern, never the actor). Compile-time registration is **explicit, not `inventory`/`linkme` life-before-main magic** (decision 2026-06-23, matches the repo's hand-wired/greppable ethos): [`registry::register_builtins`](../../crates/detection/src/registry.rs) is the single place the linked roster is named, each detector behind a Cargo feature so premium detectors stay out of open builds (§6 "selective open-sourcing"). The `Registry` dedupes on `(id, version)` — two versions of one detector coexist for safe rollout/A-B, a duplicate `(id, version)` fails the build. Value types (`DetectorId`, hand-rolled `SemVer`, `ModelKind`, `Scope`) sit alongside `events::primitives` (`DetectorRef`/`Confidence`/`AlertKind`) so detect output maps straight onto `DetectorTriggered` in task 5. `DetectionCtx`/`BlockBundle` are a deliberate skeleton — the enrichment seam task 3 fills. A reusable `MockDetector` lives behind a `test-util` feature so the task-4 detector crates test against the seam without re-rolling a double. 10 unit tests (trait dispatch, collect-in-key-order, version coexistence, duplicate rejection, runtime `register_if` gating, semver parse/order/round-trip); clippy/fmt clean (default + `test-util`). Library-only; service loop lands task 5 |
| 2 | Model registry (`id, version, config_hash, deployed_at, performance`) + per-detector feature flags (§6) | 6h | DONE — [crates/detection/src/model.rs](../../crates/detection/src/model.rs): a `ModelRegistry` catalogue of `ModelCard`s (`id`, `version`, `kind`, `scope`, `config_hash`, `deployed_at`, `performance`, `LifecycleStatus`), **kept separate** from the live `Registry` (registration ≠ cataloguing — a detector can run while `Shadow`/`Deprecated`). `ConfigHash` is a SHA-256 (`sha2`) digest of a detector's serialized config — the third leg of the `(id, version, config_hash)` `DetectorRef` each card yields, stamped onto every `DetectorTriggered` for reproducible replay (§18); `Performance` reuses the validated `Confidence` and starts `unmeasured()` (the metrics jobs in Sprint 4 t3 / Sprint 10 fill it). Per-detector **feature flags** ([flags.rs](../../crates/detection/src/flags.rs)): `FeatureFlags` (default policy + per-`DetectorId` overrides) — the *runtime* on/off that gates `register_builtins(&FeatureFlags)` via the task-1 `register_if` seam, complementing the compile-time `#[cfg(feature)]` gate. Dedupe-on-`(id, version)` builder mirrors `RegistryError`. 13 unit tests (hash stability/distinctness, ref triple, plugin-identity passthrough, version walk, dupe rejection, flag resolution); clippy/fmt clean (default + `test-util`) |
| 3 | `DetectionCtx` enrichment (token/pool/price — **no labels on hot path**, §6) | 6h | DONE — [crates/detection/src/enrichment.rs](../../crates/detection/src/enrichment.rs): the [`Enrichment`](../../crates/detection/src/enrichment.rs) layer the task-1 [`DetectionCtx`](../../crates/detection/src/ctx.rs) skeleton reserved — the *decoded* view of a block (`TokenMeta` w/ decimals, `PoolState` v2 reserves at the block, `UsdPrice` reference prices, and per-tx `TxActions` of decoded `Swap`/`TokenTransfer`) layered **additively** on the raw-facts `BlockBundle`, so detectors written against `block`/`chain`/`txs` kept compiling while gaining `ctx.enrichment()`. **Attribution-blind by construction:** every field is an on-chain fact or market quantity (address, reserve, price) — there is deliberately no `label`/`tag`/`entity` field, the §6/§8 invariant the seam was shaped to lock; the no-labels boundary is now physically enforced through enrichment, not just the bundle. `UsdPrice` is a validated newtype mirroring `Confidence` (rejects non-finite/negative — a bad oracle value surfaces at the edge, not deep in a detector); `Enrichment::usd_value(token, raw)` combines a token's `decimals` (via alloy `format_units`) with its price into the USD estimate detectors gate `min_profit_usd` on (task 4). Built once per block via the last-write-wins `EnrichmentBuilder` (**consuming `build(self)`** — maps move into the `Enrichment`, no clone) and shared by reference across the roster (§17); `DetectionCtx::new` keeps the empty-enrichment path (header-only source ⇒ detector returns nothing, not a guess), `with_enrichment` carries the decoded block. **Production hardening (code-reviewed):** `to_whole` does an allocation-free `U256→f64` widening ÷ `10^decimals` (was a `format_units` String round-trip) on the detector hot path, total over all of `U256` (no panic); `usd_value` returns `None` on a non-finite estimate so `inf` can't clear a `min_profit_usd` gate; `with_enrichment` `debug_assert!`s the enrichment describes only txs in the bundle (decode/assembly wiring bug ⇒ loud in CI, compiled out of the release hot path); `Enrichment`/`DetectionCtx` asserted `Send + Sync` for the rayon fan-out (§17). `Eq` dropped from `DetectionCtx`/`Enrichment` (f64 prices have no total equality), `PartialEq` retained. 40 detection unit tests (debug; 39 release — one debug-only panic test), incl. price validation, decimal scaling at decimals 0/18, overflow→`None`, total-over-`U256::MAX`, both-legs-needed, block-integrity invariant; clippy/fmt clean (default + `test-util`), release build green |
| 4 | Detectors: **sandwich-v1.2** and **arb-v1.0** (attribution-blind, §22 Phase 2) | 10h | DONE — first two detector crates, each an independent crate (§6) implementing `DetectorPlugin`, pure/`Scope::Block`/`ModelKind::Rule`, attribution-blind (read `DetectionCtx` facts+enrichment, **no labels**; return behaviour-only `Evidence`). **Seam extracted to [crates/detector-api/](../../crates/detector-api/)** (trait + `DetectionCtx`/enrichment + `test_util`), so the detectors depend on a small stable contract, not the heavy service crate — graph is `detection → {sandwich,arb} → detector-api ← detection`, acyclic, and a closed/premium detector needs only `detector-api` (the cleaner realization of §6's "independent crate / selective open-sourcing" once a real detector crate exposed the Cargo cycle the old optional-dep-on-`detection` template implied). `detection` re-exports `detector-api` (so `detection::DetectorPlugin` etc. unchanged) and wires the detectors in `register_builtins` as optional deps behind `sandwich`/`arb`/`detectors` features. **[sandwich-v1.2](../../crates/sandwich-detector/src/lib.rs):** same-sender frontrun/backrun bracketing ≥1 same-direction victim on one pool, opposite-direction close, net-positive base-token recovery; `min_profit_usd` gate (dust filter, valued via `usd_value`), reports unvalued-but-structural at lower confidence; greedy non-overlapping pairing. **[arb-v1.0](../../crates/arb-detector/src/lib.rs):** single-tx closed-cycle signature — exactly one token nets positive *and* cycled, every other token nets zero (rejects net-loss round-trips and open paths); two-hop + triangular, `min_profit_usd` gate. Facts-only confidence (baseline + USD-confirmed + structure lifts). 17 unit tests across the two (textbook hit, unprofitable/no-victim/different-sender rejection, USD-gate filter, unpriced fallback, single-swap/loss/open-path rejection, seam dispatch); detection gains feature-gated `register_builtins` tests. Full workspace: tests green (incl. `--all-features`), clippy 0 warnings, fmt clean |
| 5 | Emit `DetectorTriggered`, `PreliminaryAlertCreated`; in-memory cross-block state, reorg-versioned (§6, §15) | 5h | DONE — the **synchronous emission core**, split pure-mapping/effectful-shell exactly like ingestion's `tree.rs`→`pipeline.rs` (the async/Kafka loop + rayon fan-out is Sprint 4 t2, the event-driven rewind Sprint 4 t1). **[emit.rs](../../crates/detection/src/emit.rs):** pure mapping functions returning `Vec<DomainEvent>` (no envelopes/async/IO, `assert_eq!`-testable, backtest-replayable) — `detector_triggered`/`preliminary_alert`/`implicated_addresses` composed by [`DetectionPlan::detection_events`](../../crates/detection/src/emit.rs), which fans the roster over one `DetectionCtx` (key order) and emits each finding's `DetectorTriggered` immediately followed by its provisional `PreliminaryAlertCreated` (causal trigger→alert pairing). **"Parse, don't validate" boundary:** `DetectionPlan::link(&Registry, &ModelRegistry)` pairs every live detector with its exact `(id, version, config_hash)` `DetectorRef` (task 2) **once at boot**, returning `UnlinkedDetector` if any live detector is uncatalogued — so the per-block emit path is *total* (no hot-path lookup, no fabricated `config_hash`, no degraded skip). Fail-fast at startup, the same discipline as `register_builtins` panicking on a duplicate — going live with a detector whose evidence can't be reproduced (§18) is worse than not booting. **Attribution-blind end-to-end:** the alert's `addresses` are the `from`/`to` of the implicated txs pulled from enrichment — on-chain *facts*, deduped first-seen, **never labels** (§8) — and a header-only source yields none rather than a guess. **[state.rs](../../crates/detection/src/state.rs):** `CrossBlockState<S>`, the reorg-versioned in-memory store a future `Scope::CrossBlock` detector accumulates into (both built-ins are `Scope::Block`, so this is the typed home its first writer — wash-trading — will use, mirroring `Performance` preceding its metrics job). One **snapshot per applied canonical block** (ascending `VecDeque`), so rewind-to-common-ancestor is just discarding snapshots above it — no undo-log replay-ordering subtlety (the block tree already solved that once). `apply` (ascending-order `debug_assert!`) + window prune (the tip is always retained; pruned blocks are beyond reorg reach per §15), `revert_tip` (tip-conditional, composes with the tip-first `BlockReverted` stream), `rewind_to(ancestor)` (the §15 one-step rollback). 15 new unit tests (triple/txs/confidence/detail carried; provisional alert w/ fresh ids; address dedup+order+empty-enrichment+contract-creation; trigger→alert pairing in roster order w/ exact triple; no-findings⇒no-events; `link` fails on an uncatalogued live detector; apply/revert/rewind/window/reorg-lifecycle/zero-window). Full workspace green (incl. `--all-features`), clippy 0 warnings, fmt clean, release build green |

**Deliverable:** `BlockAssembled` → `PreliminaryAlertCreated` in < 1s; every `DetectorTriggered` carries exact `(id, version, config_hash)`.
**Status:** all tasks done — the plugin seam + compile-time registry (code-reviewed: explicit feature-gated registration over `inventory` magic, named-field errors, dead API pruned, shared `test_util` mock), the model registry + per-detector feature flags (separate catalogue, SHA-256 `config_hash`, `Confidence`-validated `Performance`, runtime flags gating `register_builtins`), the `DetectionCtx` enrichment layer (attribution-blind token/pool/price + decoded per-tx swaps/transfers, validated `UsdPrice`, `usd_value` USD estimate, additive over the task-1 skeleton), the first two detectors — `sandwich-v1.2` + `arb-v1.0` — with the seam extracted to a standalone `detector-api` crate so detectors decouple from the service (acyclic, premium detectors need only the thin contract), and the task-5 synchronous emission core (`emit::detection_events` mapping findings→`DetectorTriggered`/`PreliminaryAlertCreated` with the exact `DetectorRef` triple, attribution-blind addresses from enrichment; `state::CrossBlockState` reorg-versioned snapshot store + rewind primitive). The async/Kafka consumer loop, rayon fan-out, and the event-driven `BlockReverted` rewind that drives `CrossBlockState` carry into Sprint 4 (tasks 1–2).

**Carry-over (schema-hardening, from Sprint 1, agreed 2026-06-19):** (b) upcasting-seam note in [crates/events/SCHEMA.md](../../crates/events/SCHEMA.md) — **DONE (2026-06-23):** documents the read path for old events after the first incompatible `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump (deserialize → version-tagged raw → `v1→v2→…` upcast chain at the `from_json_slice` seam; YAGNI at v1, so captured-as-intent not built). (a) `proptest` round-trip for `DomainEvent` (codec totality) is **moved to Sprint 4 task 4**, where `proptest` enters the workspace anyway.

### Sprint 4 — Detection hardening + reorg rollback (Week 8-9) — DONE

**Goal:** Detection is correct under reorgs and ready to feed simulation.

| # | Task | Est | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Rewind cross-block detector state to common ancestor on `BlockReverted` (§15) | 6h | DONE — [crates/detection/src/reorg.rs](../../crates/detection/src/reorg.rs): the thin **consumer** on top of task-5's `CrossBlockState` primitive that rolls a cross-block detector's state back to the common ancestor from the `BlockReverted` event stream. `apply_reverts(&mut dyn Rewindable, &[BlockReverted])` replays the stream **tip-first via `revert_tip`** (not `rewind_to`): a reorg reaches detection as one `BlockReverted` per orphaned block, top-down, and the common ancestor is *implicit* (one below the lowest reverted height) and never named in the event — so popping each orphan in turn is the honest reconstruction over the seam. **Resilient to an at-least-once stream:** `revert_tip` is tip-conditional, so a stale/duplicate/aged-out revert is ignored (counted in `ReorgRewind.ignored`) rather than popping the wrong version, and a gapped stream under-rewinds (detector rebuilds from the re-applied branch) rather than corrupting — idempotent under redelivery, which is what lets the scheduler replay each Kafka `BlockReverted` as a one-element stream (`apply_reverts(slice::from_ref(&reverted))`). Disorder *within* the stream is the one thing not tolerated — tip-first is a checked invariant (`assert_tip_first` `debug_assert!`, mirroring `CrossBlockState::apply`'s ascending guard). **`Rewindable`** erases the detector state type `S` (a reorg only moves the tip, never reads `S`), blanket-impl'd for every `CrossBlockState<S>`, so **`CrossBlockStates`** — the `(id, version)`-keyed roster, plural analogue of `Registry` — holds every cross-block detector's heterogeneous state behind one trait object and rewinds them all in a single `apply_reverts` (a `RosterRewind` aggregates per-detector outcomes, which legitimately differ when a shorter window already aged a block out). Wired into the scheduler (task 2): `BlockEvent::Reverted` → `CrossBlockStates::apply_reverts`, publishing nothing. Roster is empty at runtime today (both built-ins are `Scope::Block`) — the typed home before its first `CrossBlock` writer (wash-trading). 15 reorg unit tests (empty/single/redelivered-idempotent/leading-stale/gapped streams, trait-object dispatch, replay-back-to-common-ancestor, whole-roster rewind w/ differing per-detector outcomes, mis-ordered `debug_assert` trip, observe→snapshot→detect in key order) + scheduler revert test; clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean |
| 2 | Concurrency model: async scheduler, per-block rayon fan-out, bounded channels (§17) | 6h | DONE — the detection service's **effectful shell** ([crates/detection/src/scheduler.rs](../../crates/detection/src/scheduler.rs)) + runnable binary ([main.rs](../../crates/detection/src/main.rs)/[config.rs](../../crates/detection/src/config.rs)). Three tasks joined by **two bounded `mpsc` channels** (work, commit) for inter-stage backpressure + one `CancellationToken`: a Kafka `run_consumer` decoding `BlockAssembled`→header-only `DetectionCtx` / `BlockReverted`→revert, the async `Scheduler` (single writer over the one boot-`link`ed `DetectionPlan` + cross-block roster), and a `run_committer` advancing offsets only after a block is durably published (at-least-once; dedup on `event_id`). **Per-block rayon fan-out** ([emit.rs](../../crates/detection/src/emit.rs) `detection_events_parallel`) runs the pure `Block` detectors on rayon via `spawn_blocking` (never CPU on the reactor, §17), order-identical to the sequential reference (asserted). The **cross-block per-block apply path** `state.rs`/`reorg.rs` deferred to here is complete: a new object-safe `CrossBlockDetector` seam ([crates/detector-api/src/cross_block.rs](../../crates/detector-api/src/cross_block.rs)) threaded through `detect`, an erased `CrossBlockSlot`/`Slot<D>` ([reorg.rs](../../crates/detection/src/reorg.rs)) that observes→snapshots→detects and rewinds, driven serially by the scheduler and rewound on reorg via task-1's `apply_reverts`. The shared Kafka **publish seam** was extracted to a new [crates/event-bus](../../crates/event-bus/src/lib.rs) crate (`EventSink`/`KafkaEventSink`/`publish_resilient`), now shared by ingestion + detection. 116 unit tests green (incl. seq/parallel equivalence, scheduler loop + backpressure + shutdown, slot observe/detect/rewind), clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean. **Note:** `BlockAssembled` carries no tx hashes/enrichment and the live source is header-only, so live detectors no-op until a tx-carrying source/decoding lands — the *plumbing* is end-to-end; meaningful yields are a follow-up. No `Scope::CrossBlock` detector ships yet, so that roster is empty at runtime (typed home before its first writer) |
| 3 | Per-detector metrics: hit rate, latency (§19) | 3h | DONE — the first application-metrics wiring in the codebase, built on the `metrics` facade + a Prometheus exporter. **Shared seam** in [crates/telemetry/src/metrics.rs](../../crates/telemetry/src/metrics.rs): `telemetry::metrics::init(addr)` installs the global recorder + serves `/metrics` over an HTTP listener — the metrics counterpart to `telemetry::init`, so every future service exports the same way (§19; `metrics-exporter-prometheus`, `http-listener` only, push-gateway trimmed). **Per-detector recording** in [crates/detection/src/metrics.rs](../../crates/detection/src/metrics.rs): one `record_detector_run(&DetectorRef, elapsed, findings)` emitting `detector_runs_total` / `detector_hits_total` / `detector_findings_total` (counters) + `detector_detect_duration_seconds` (histogram), all labeled `(detector, version)` — **hit rate is derived in PromQL** (`hits / runs`) from two monotonic counters rather than stored, so it survives restarts and re-aggregates across instances; `config_hash` is deliberately **not** a label (cardinality blowup on every redeploy — it lives on the events, §18). Recorded at the **single instrumented seam call site** `LinkedDetector::detect` ([emit.rs](../../crates/detection/src/emit.rs)) that **both** `Block` paths (sequential reference + rayon `detection_events_parallel`) route through, plus the cross-block slot ([reorg.rs](../../crates/detection/src/reorg.rs) — `detect` only, `observe` excluded), so the numbers can't drift between paths and a detector can't silently vanish from the dashboard (coverage total by construction, like link-or-fail). The facade is a near-free no-op until the binary installs the exporter, so the detection *library*/replay/backtests stay exporter-agnostic — recording never changes the events produced. Binary wires it in [main.rs](../../crates/detection/src/main.rs) before the first block, on a configurable `DETECTION_METRICS_ADDR` (default `0.0.0.0:9100`, [config.rs](../../crates/detection/src/config.rs)). 4 new tests (hit/miss/accumulation via a scoped `metrics-util` `DebuggingRecorder` — note its `snapshot()` *drains*, so it's read once; + an emit-path wiring test proving a run-per-detector and hit-only-on-finding); clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean, `cargo deny` (licenses/bans) ok |
| 4 | Detection unit + property tests (`proptest`) on known historical MEV blocks | 6h | DONE — `proptest = "1"` added to workspace deps (dev-dependency only). **Detector property tests** ([crates/sandwich-detector/tests/property.rs](../../crates/sandwich-detector/tests/property.rs), [crates/arb-detector/tests/property.rs](../../crates/arb-detector/tests/property.rs)): a profitable MEV pattern *planted* in an arbitrary block of "noise" swaps at an arbitrary position is **always found** (recall — the property-test analogue of a known-MEV block with ground truth: the sandwich is isolated on its own pool's swap bucket, the arb is a per-tx decision so noise can't mask it); **every emitted finding is structurally valid** cross-checked against the ctx (sandwich: front/back share the reported attacker as sender, victims sit between with a different sender, `txs == [front, victims…, back]`; arb: re-derives net-per-token and proves exactly one token nets positive == `profit_token` & both spent+received, all others net zero, profit == net); `detect` is **deterministic**; and the **profit gate filters, not the structure** (arb gate proven *monotone by tx*, sandwich planted pattern suppressed above its profit / reported below). Plus **realistic mainnet-shaped regression fixtures** with real WETH/USDC/DAI addresses + plausible magnitudes (no recorded-block replay harness exists — detectors are pure fns of decoded enrichment, built directly via `CtxBuilder`). **Also cleared carried-over schema follow-up (a)** ([crates/events/tests/proptest_round_trip.rs](../../crates/events/tests/proptest_round_trip.rs)): hand-written strategies over every `DomainEvent` variant + envelope assert `from_json_slice(to_json_vec(x)) == x` (codec totality), complementing `wire_format.rs`'s byte-exact goldens. **Gotcha:** `serde_json`'s shortest-`f64` repr is non-injective & non-idempotent, so exact-equality round trips over `f64` fields require pinning floats to round-trip fixed points (see the test's module docs). fmt + clippy (`--all-targets --all-features`) clean; green at `PROPTEST_CASES=2000` |

| 1 | Dispatcher: thin Kafka consumer on `PreliminaryAlertCreated` → publishes `SimulationJob` **command** to RabbitMQ (§7) | 4h | DONE — new **[`simulation`](../../crates/simulation/)** crate, the slow path's front half, split pure-core / I/O-shell like detection's `scheduler.rs`. **[command.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/command.rs):** the `SimulationJob` **command** (deliberately *not* a `DomainEvent` — it lives only on RabbitMQ, never the event store, §2/§7) + `Priority(0..=9)` + the pure `job_for_alert` mapping returning `(SimulationJob, SimulationRequested)` — the command to queue and the audit event to emit alongside it so the *request* is auditable even though the command isn't (§7). Priority is derived from confidence as a documented placeholder (tier/profit-based is a follow-up needing billing context, same shape as detection's boot-placeholder `config_hash`). **[queue.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/queue.rs):** the `JobSink` publish seam — the RabbitMQ analogue of `event-bus`'s `EventSink` — with the lapin-backed `RabbitJobSink` (publisher **confirms** for at-least-once; persistent + per-message priority) and `publish_resilient` (transient-retry / permanent-skip, mirroring the Kafka producer policy). Object-safe so the dispatcher is unit-testable against an in-memory double with no broker. **[dispatcher.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/dispatcher.rs):** `Dispatcher` consumes `PreliminaryAlertCreated`, queues the command, emits the `SimulationRequested` audit event, then commits — **at-least-once**, command-first so a successful audit fact implies a real dispatch; a `Disposition` enum cleanly separates *commit* (dispatched, or poison-skipped) from *abandon* (shutdown — leave the offset for redelivery), closing a poison-job hot-loop. No committer stage / per-chain ordering (jobs are independent, §7). Queue *topology* (quorum, priority args, DLX) deferred to t2 — the sink publishes to `sim.jobs` by name so it can't conflict with that declare. 14 unit tests (priority clamp/derive; mapping projects the alert + keys both outputs by `alert_id`; job JSON round-trip; resilient retry/give-up-on-shutdown; dispatch queues+audits; abandon-on-shutdown; poison-skip regression guard; envelope decode/topic). Full workspace green, clippy 0 warnings, fmt clean, `cargo deny` clean (new `lapin` subtree). |
| 2 | RabbitMQ `sim.jobs`: quorum queue, durable, priority 0–9, dead-letter exchange `sim.jobs.dlx` (§7, §20) | 5h | DONE — the one-time queue **topology declaration** the t1 sink was written against (its module docs deferred the declare to here). New **[topology.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/topology.rs):** `declare_sim_topology(url, cfg)` declares, in order, the DLX (`sim.jobs.dlx`, durable **fanout**) → a durable **quorum** dead-letter queue (`sim.jobs.dlq`) bound behind it → the `sim.jobs` work queue (durable **quorum**, `x-dead-letter-exchange=sim.jobs.dlx`, `x-delivery-limit=N`). Idempotent (re-declare with identical args is a no-op; conflicting args fail the channel = fail-fast on topology drift). Called once at boot in [main.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/main.rs) before the sink connects. **Quorum-vs-priority decision (the one place the spec couldn't be taken literally):** on RabbitMQ 4 a quorum queue **rejects `x-max-priority`**, and full 0–9 priority needs a *classic* queue — but classic queues aren't replicated (mirrored queues removed in RMQ4), forfeiting §20's HA. Chose **quorum** (HA + native `x-delivery-limit`→DLX, which is exactly §7's "fails N times → DLX" — a feature classic queues lack); priority rides the quorum queue's built-in **2-level** high(`>4`)/normal(`≤4`) split on the still-stamped 0–9 message property. `x-max-priority` deliberately omitted; the t1 [command.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/command.rs) `Priority` doc was corrected from its stale "declared with `x-max-priority=9`" claim. **[config.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/config.rs):** `RabbitConfig` gains `dlx`/`dead_letter_queue`/`delivery_limit` (env `RABBITMQ_SIM_DLX`/`_DLQ`/`_DELIVERY_LIMIT`, defaults `sim.jobs.dlx`/`sim.jobs.dlq`/`5`); shared `amqp_connect` helper extracted in [queue.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/queue.rs) so sink + topology share one connection setup. **Tests:** 4 pure unit tests on the arg builders (quorum + DLX + delivery-limit present; `x-max-priority` **absent**; configurable limit; DLQ terminal) + a live-broker **integration test** ([tests/topology.rs](../../crates/simulation/tests/topology.rs), `#[ignore]`, testcontainers RabbitMQ, run via `just test-integration`): declares-idempotently-and-routes-a-published-job (publish via `RabbitJobSink` → `basic_get` → body round-trips, priority property intact) + a **decision guard** proving the broker rejects `x-max-priority` on a quorum queue. **Bug fixed:** `RABBITMQ_URL` ended in a bare `/` = the *empty* vhost (broker rejects `vhost not found`); the default vhost must be `%2f` — corrected in `.env`/`.env.example` (the dispatcher's first real broker run surfaced this latent bug). **Observability (§19, "add as built"):** RabbitMQ's `rabbitmq_prometheus` plugin (already enabled, :15692) wired into the stack — new `rabbitmq` scrape job in [deploy/prometheus.yml](../../deploy/prometheus.yml) (`/metrics/per-object` for per-`queue` labels, reachable by service name on the `backend` net) + auto-provisioned Grafana dashboard [deploy/grafana/dashboards/simulation.json](../../deploy/grafana/dashboards/simulation.json) (backlog/ready · DLQ depth w/ red-on-`>0` · ready-vs-unacked over time · publish/deliver/ack rates · consumers). **Verified end-to-end:** dispatcher boots → topology declared → consumed the existing `PreliminaryAlertCreated` backlog → `sim.jobs` depth climbs (no t3 worker draining yet = a live backpressure demo), DLQ 0, series scraping into the dashboard. clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean; workspace green |
| 3 | Worker pool: revm on rayon, competing consumers, per-job ack/redelivery (§7, §17) | 8h | DONE — the slow path's **back half**: a separate **[`simulation-worker`](../../crates/simulation/src/bin/worker.rs)** binary (own `[[bin]]`, so it scales independently of the dispatcher — §20 "scale aggressively, revm CPU is the bottleneck") that drains `sim.jobs` with competing consumers, runs **real revm on a shared rayon pool** off the async reactor (§17), publishes the result back onto Kafka, and acks per job. Split pure-engine / I/O-shell like the rest of the crate. **[consumer.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/consumer.rs):** the `JobSource` **consume seam** (the competing-consumer analogue of t1's `JobSink`) — `RabbitJobSource` does a lapin `basic_consume` with **manual ack** (`no_ack=false`) + a bounded **`basic_qos` prefetch** (queue depth stays the backpressure signal, §17); an undecodable body is dead-lettered at the source (can never become a job). `JobDelivery` carries the decoded job + a `DeliveryAck` handle behind one `settle(Disposition)` verb — the single place the §7 dispositions (`Ack`/`Requeue`/`DeadLetter`) map onto AMQP (`ack` / `nack requeue` / `nack→DLX`). **[simulator.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/simulator.rs):** the **revm 41 engine** (`Simulator` seam + `RevmSimulator`) — seeds a `CacheDB<EmptyDB>` from a self-contained `SimulationRequest` (block env + seeded accounts + tx bundle), replays the bundle gas-free (`gas_price=0`), and **diffs balances** → attacker profit / victim loss → confirm/retract (§7 "what simulation confirms"). Pure scenario→outcome, so a unit test seeds two accounts and asserts the profit with no RPC. Typed `SimError::{Transient,Poison}` (a malformed/hostile tx the EVM rejects is poison; a db fault is transient) so t4's gas/step caps slot in as new poison cases without reshaping callers. **[worker.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/worker.rs):** `Worker::process` (the **core**: resolve → simulate on the rayon pool via a oneshot bridge → publish → return `Disposition`) + `Worker::run` (the drain loop, `select!` on shutdown). **Per-job ack/redelivery (§7), the crux:** ack only **after** the result is durably published; a transient fault (or shutdown mid-publish) **requeues** (at-least-once, safe because results are `alert_id`-keyed and dedup downstream); poison **dead-letters** to the DLX rather than loops; a worker that *crashes* mid-run never acks → the quorum queue redelivers, and `x-delivery-limit` (t2) is the crash-loop backstop. **[result.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/result.rs):** pure `SimulationOutcome → {SimulationCompleted, +IncidentCreated when confirmed}` mapping, `alert_id`-keyed. **Decisions (with the user):** the **revm engine is real and complete** now, but two pieces are the one acknowledged seam — **[resolver.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/resolver.rs)** `JobResolver` (turning a job's alert into a runnable `(block, tx_set)` scenario) ships a documented **stub** (`UnresolvedJobResolver` → poison → DLX), because `SimulationJob`/`PreliminaryAlertCreated` carry no txs (only `DetectorTriggered` does) and the event-store has no by-alert read API yet; and **fork-from-chain** is deferred because revm's `AlloyDB` feature pins `alloy-provider` 2.0, conflicting with ingestion's 1.x — so the live fork + bundle resolution land with that bump / by-alert query (Sprint 6). The *plumbing* is end-to-end; the live yield is the follow-up — same honesty as detection t2's header-only no-op. revm pinned `default-features=false` (precompiles enumerated, `alloydb` off, `tracer` dropped) — `cargo deny` clean, no new allowances. **Clean-code pass (code-reviewed against the new [engineering-conventions.md](../../docs/engineering-conventions.md)):** `DeliveryAck` collapsed 3 methods → one `settle(Disposition)`; `disposition_for` DRYs the transient→requeue/poison→dead-letter rule; **`MinProfit`** validated newtype (rejects negative/NaN at boot, mirrors `UsdPrice`) replaces a raw `f64` threshold; severity bands named consts; shared doubles extracted to **[test_util.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/test_util.rs)** behind a `test-util` feature (unit + integration share one set, mirroring `detector-api`). **Tests:** 31 unit (engine balance-diff confirm/retract/empty/poison; worker ack/requeue/dead-letter/shutdown dispositions; result mapping; resolver stub) + a live-broker **integration suite** ([tests/worker.rs](../../crates/simulation/tests/worker.rs), `#[ignore]`, testcontainers RabbitMQ): a worker **acks a job off the queue**, a worker that **dies mid-run triggers redelivery** (unacked → requeued, `redelivered` flag set), and a **poison job dead-letters** onto `sim.jobs.dlq` instead of looping — the t3 deliverable proven end-to-end. clippy `-D warnings` (+ `--all-features`) + fmt clean, both binaries build, workspace green. |
| 4 | Simulation hardening: cap gas/steps, sandbox revm, cache by `(block, tx_set)` — treat bytecode as hostile (§7) | 6h | DONE — three hardening layers on the t3 engine, all in the `simulation` crate, treating honeypot bytecode as hostile input (§7). **Cap gas/steps ([simulator.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/simulator.rs)):** new `SimLimits { per_tx_gas, bundle_gas_budget }`. **Key decision:** the step cap rides revm's *native gas meter* (every opcode costs ≥1 gas) rather than a tracing `Inspector` — the workspace `Cargo.toml` deliberately drops revm's `tracer` subtree, and re-adding it for a step counter would bloat the `cargo deny` surface for no gain. Each tx's `gas_limit` is **clamped** to `per_tx_gas` (default 30M = one mainnet block), so a contract that loops forever exhausts its budget and **halts `OutOfGas`** — a *valid* "no value extracted" outcome (profit 0, not confirmed), not an error, consistent with the engine's existing "reverts/halts are valid" stance. The bundle's cumulative `tx_gas_used()` is held under `bundle_gas_budget` (default 90M ≈ a few txs; a sandwich is three); exceeding it is **`SimError::Poison`** (deterministic on retry → dead-letter, not requeue), bounding a pathological many-tx bundle the per-tx clamp alone wouldn't catch. **Sandbox revm:** the engine run is wrapped in `std::panic::catch_unwind` (`run_sandboxed`) — a panic provoked by malformed bytecode becomes poison rather than unwinding the rayon worker thread (verified no `panic=abort` in any profile, so unwinding works); state already runs over `EmptyDB`, so there's no chain I/O to escape to. **Cache by `(block, tx_set)` (new [cache.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/cache.rs)):** `CachingSimulator<S>` **decorator** implementing `Simulator` — keeps `RevmSimulator` a pure function (memoization is the stateful layer, separately tested), the worker still erases to `Arc<dyn Simulator>`. Key is `(block.number, sorted txs)` — order-independent set; a hit returns the cached *financial verdict* **re-stamped** with the requesting scenario's identity (`alert_id`/`kind`/`txs`), so two alerts over the same bundle each carry their own downstream dedup key over shared compute. This is the §7 idempotency the work queue leans on: a redelivered job (RabbitMQ at-least-once) or a bundle re-hit during backtest replay is a cache hit, not duplicate revm work. **FIFO-bounded** to a configurable capacity — an unbounded map is itself a memory-DoS vector (flood unique bundles), the opposite of treating input as hostile; errors are never memoized (a transient fault can still succeed on redelivery). **Wiring:** `RevmSimulator::new` keeps its single-arg signature (defaults) + new `with_limits`; generalized `impl Simulator for Arc<dyn Simulator>` → blanket `impl<S: Simulator + ?Sized> for Arc<S>`; [config.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/config.rs) gains `sim_limits` + `cache_capacity` (env `SIMULATION_PER_TX_GAS`/`_BUNDLE_GAS_BUDGET`/`_CACHE_CAPACITY`, default cap 1024); [bin/worker.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/bin/worker.rs) stacks `CachingSimulator::new(RevmSimulator::with_limits(...), cap)`. **Tests:** +10 unit (per-tx cap halts a real `JUMPDEST;PUSH1 0;JUMP` infinite-loop bytecode as a valid outcome; bundle-budget trip is poison; sandbox maps panic→poison; cache hit-skips-engine, distinct-key miss, tx-order-independence, identity rebind, FIFO eviction, zero-cap passthrough, errors-not-cached) — 41 lib tests green. clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean; the doc seam `SimError::Poison` updated (gas-budget + caught-panic now named poison causes). |

**Deliverable:** A provisional alert dispatches a job; a worker runs revm and acks; a killed mid-run worker triggers redelivery; a poison job dead-letters instead of looping.

### Sprint 6 — Simulation results → incidents (Week 12-13) — DONE

**Goal:** Confirmed incidents re-enter the event backbone, idempotently and reorg-safe.

| # | Task | Est | Status |

| 1 | Compute attacker profit, victim loss, counterfactual, honeypot check (§7 "what simulation confirms") | 8h | DONE — the revm engine now computes all **four** §7 confirmations, not just the single balance-diff of t3. Modelled as an honest **`Scenario` enum** on `SimulationRequest` (replacing the flat `bundle`/`attacker`/`victim` fields — the resolver picks the variant from `AlertKind`), a closed sum type matched exhaustively (the idiomatic Rust choice over a `dyn Strategy` trait; matches the crate's `SimError`/`Disposition` taste). **[simulator.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/simulator.rs):** three arms over the same seeded pre-state — **`ValueExtraction { bundle, attacker, victim }`** (attacker profit + victim loss by one balance diff — t3's behaviour, preserved; arbitrage/generic extraction); **`Sandwich { bundle, attacker, victim, victim_swap }`** the **counterfactual** — replay the full attack for attacker profit, then re-simulate the victim's swap *alone* against the untouched pre-state (frontrun removed) and take victim loss as how much better the victim did unsandwiched (the harm caused, not the raw balance move); **`Honeypot { prober, buy, sell }`** — buy then sell from a fresh funded address, confirm iff the buy succeeds and the sell reverts/halts (read off revm's per-tx `ExecutionResult::is_success()`, no token accounting), reporting the prober's trapped ETH as the loss. **Structure:** the t3/t4 seed→build→replay→gas-cap→read-balances body factored into one private `run()` helper reused by every arm (so the §7 gas caps + panic sandbox apply uniformly and no unnameable revm types leak); a private `Verdict` separates "the money a strategy decided" from the identity `simulate_inner` stamps onto the uniform `SimulationOutcome`, so `result.rs`/cache/worker seams are **untouched**. **Clean-code pass (self-reviewed):** `RunResult` returns address-keyed `ProbedBalance`s with intent-named accessors (`gain`/`loss`/`balance_after`) instead of `pre[0]`/`post[1]` positional indexing (killed the "probe order matters" hazard); the shared confirm-above-threshold + severity-band rule DRY'd into one `profit_verdict` helper (same move as the existing `severity_for`/`disposition_for`). **Measurement depth (decided with the user):** kept the engine's gas-free **ETH-balance-diff** model + execution-result honeypot signal — no ERC20/`balanceOf` token accounting (documented placeholder, same stance as the `severity_for` bands). **Scope:** pure-engine only, exactly like t3/t4 — the `JobResolver` that would build these scenarios from live chain state stays the documented stub (still gated on the alloy-provider bump / by-alert event-store query); event schema (`SimulationCompleted`/`IncidentCreated` still carry profit/victim_loss/confirmed) untouched — emission is t2. **Tests:** +5 pure synthetic (sandwich counterfactual harm via a hand-rolled `CALL(caller, SELFBALANCE)` drain contract; sandwich no-harm → 0 loss; honeypot `CALLVALUE`-branch token buy-ok/sell-reverts → confirmed High; clean token both-succeed → not confirmed; buy-itself-reverts → not confirmed), existing engine tests reworked onto `ValueExtraction` with behaviour preserved — **46 lib tests green**. clippy `-D warnings` (+ `--all-features`) + fmt clean; both binaries build. |
| 2 | Emit `SimulationCompleted`, `IncidentCreated`, `IncidentRetracted`, `IncidentFinalized` on Kafka keyed by `alert_id` (§7) | 4h | DONE — the **result-path keying** the §7 idempotent projection (t3) leans on. Previously *every* event was chain-keyed ([event-bus](../../crates/event-bus/src/lib.rs) hard-coded `envelope.chain.id()`), so a redelivered `SimulationCompleted` could land on any partition — no per-incident dedup key. The fix is a single classification on the schema, not the producer: new **[`DomainEvent::result_partition_key`](../../crates/events/src/lib.rs)** (exhaustive match, sibling to `incident_id`/`addresses`) returns the confirm/retract lifecycle's **incident business key** as a typed **`PartitionKey`** sum type (`Chain`/`Alert`/`Incident` — not a bare `String` where a chain `"1"` and a UUID are indistinguishable; `Display` is the sole wire rendering and centralises the "chain key is `.id()`, not `chain-1`" subtlety) — `PartitionKey::Alert(alert_id)` while the incident is still tied to its provisional alert (`SimulationCompleted`/`IncidentCreated`, co-partitioned so they dedup+order together), then `PartitionKey::Incident(incident_id)` once the alert is upgraded and only the incident is named (`IncidentRetracted`/`IncidentFinalized`) — and `None` (→ chain default) for everything else. **No schema change:** the spec (§2 lines 58–59) keeps `IncidentRetracted{incident_id,reason}`/`IncidentFinalized{incident_id,block_hash}` `alert_id`-free and §7 keys the path "by `alert_id`/incident", commutative at the projection — so the two id spaces share one keyspace by design and the locked wire-format/proptest goldens are untouched. **`EventEnvelope::partition_key`** applies it (result key when present, else chain), and [`KafkaEventSink::publish`](../../crates/event-bus/src/lib.rs) derives the record key from it — the one place keying lives, so producers can't drift. `SimulationRequested` deliberately stays chain-keyed (dispatcher's audit fact, not the result path). The worker already emits `SimulationCompleted`/`IncidentCreated` (S5t3) — they now key correctly for free; the `IncidentRetracted`/`IncidentFinalized` **triggers** are t4 (`BlockReverted` retract) + finality, and land keyed via the same seam. +3 unit tests (all four keyed by their business key; chain + `SimulationRequested` fall back to chain; envelope prefers the result key over chain). clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean; workspace green. |
| 3 | Idempotent result path: duplicate `SimulationCompleted` is a no-op; ordering reasserted at projection (§7) | 5h | DONE — the **consumer counterpart** to t2's result-path keying: the pure incident **read-model projection** ([crates/simulation/src/projection.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/projection.rs)) that folds the result lifecycle back into one coherent incident row, idempotently and order-independently. `IncidentProjection::apply(&EventEnvelope) → Applied{Updated,Duplicate,Ignored}` is a deterministic function of the **set** of events seen, not their arrival order — so no exactly-once machinery is needed, exactly §7's "order is reasserted at the projection, not demanded of the queue". **A CRDT-shaped fold**, each `IncidentRecord` field with its own merge rule so re-applying/reordering always converges: **`status`** climbs a monotonic ladder `Unconfirmed < Confirmed < Finalized < Retracted` (`rank()`) and never regresses — a redelivered lower-lifecycle event can't demote a row, and **retraction is the authoritative last word** (outranks finality: a §7/§15 reorg or contradicting run withdraws an incident even post-finalization, documented as the one deliberate ordering call); **monetary figures** are **last-writer-by-event-time** on an `occurred_at` watermark (`figures_at`), so a re-sim with newer numbers overwrites while an older/redelivered one is dropped — a deterministic re-run reports identical figures and is observably a **no-op** (the §7 duplicate); **identity** (`incident_id`/`kind`/`severity`/`txs`) is **set-once** from `IncidentCreated`. **The two-keyspace correlation, the crux:** `SimulationCompleted`/`IncidentCreated` are alert-keyed but `IncidentRetracted`/`IncidentFinalized` are incident-keyed (t2), so rows key by the `alert_id` that spans the whole lifecycle, `IncidentCreated` teaches the `incident_id → alert_id` link, and a **terminal event that overtakes its `IncidentCreated` across partitions is buffered as an orphan** and replayed onto the row the moment the link appears — so nothing is lost and order is reasserted even across keyspaces. Pure/in-memory (no Kafka, no DB), `assert_eq!`-testable like [result.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/result.rs)/detection's `emit` — persistence (Postgres confirmed incidents + ClickHouse analytics projection) is t5, plugging a store in **behind** this fold so the idempotency+ordering guarantees live in one tested place. **10 unit tests:** completed+created fold to one row; **duplicate `SimulationCompleted` (fresh-id rerun *and* exact redelivery) is a no-op**; figures last-writer-by-event-time (newer wins, late-older dropped); unconfirmed records an audit outcome without an incident; status never regresses; retraction outranks finalization (audit of both kept); **terminal-before-creation reordered via the orphan buffer**; duplicate orphan is a no-op; non-result events ignored; and **`the_fold_is_order_independent`** — the four lifecycle events over several permutations (incl. terminals-first) converge to an identical row. **Production hardening:** the terminal-before-create orphan buffer is **FIFO-bounded** (`OrphanBuffer`, `DEFAULT_ORPHAN_CAPACITY`/`with_orphan_capacity`) — an unbounded orphan map is the same memory-exhaustion/DoS vector [cache.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/cache.rs) guards (a flood of `IncidentRetracted`/`IncidentFinalized` for never-created incidents), so it evicts the oldest orphaned incident at capacity and **logs a warning** (ops alarms on a non-draining orphan set = a stalled upstream partition); `by_alert` (the read model) is deliberately **not** in-memory-evicted (durability/scale is t5's store, not a lossy cap — documented, not faked); `apply` **never panics** — a would-be dangling `incident→alert` link is logged and no-op'd rather than crashing the consumer (§4 "one event must never wedge the stream"); and `orphan_len()`/`len()` + the `Applied` verdict are exposed for the consuming shell to export as metrics (§19), keeping the fold exporter-agnostic. clippy `-D warnings` (+ `--all-features`) + fmt clean; 58 simulation lib tests green (incl. bounded-eviction + drain-frees-slot), workspace green. |
| 4 | Cancel pending jobs + retract incidents on `BlockReverted` (§7, §15) | 4h | DONE — the simulation service's two `BlockReverted` reactions (§15), split pure-core/effectful-shell like the rest of the crate ([crates/simulation/src/reorg.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/reorg.rs)). **Cancel pending jobs — the §7 "generation check on the consumer":** §7 offers `basic.reject`-no-requeue *or* a generation check; we take the latter because a `SimulationJob` carries no block (nothing to reject *by block* on the queue), but a worker learns the block the moment it **resolves** the job. **`OrphanedBlocks`** is the pure, lock-free generation-check state — a set of orphaned block **hashes** (reorg-safe identity: a reorg replaces the orphan at height N with a *different* block at N, so matching by height would wrongly cancel the canonical replacement), **FIFO-bounded** (`DEFAULT_ORPHAN_BLOCK_CAPACITY`, the same [cache.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/cache.rs)/orphan-buffer DoS discipline — a `BlockReverted` flood can't grow it unbounded; a block old enough to evict has no pending jobs left). Read behind a small object-safe **`OrphanGuard`** seam (`NeverOrphaned` disables it, mirroring the `JobResolver`/`Simulator` seams); **`SharedOrphanedBlocks`** is the `Mutex`-wrapped concurrent view. Integrated into **`Worker::process`** right after resolve: if `request.block_ref()` is orphaned, the job is obsolete → **ack (drop), publish nothing** (a reorg-cancel is expected, not poison — no DLX noise, no phantom incident for an orphaned block). Each worker replica feeds its own set via a **broadcast** `BlockReverted` consumer (`run_revert_tracker` + `build_broadcast_consumer`: unique-per-process group + `latest`, since every replica must see every revert). Needed a reorg-safe block identity on the resolved scenario: added `hash` to `BlockParams` (defaulted `B256::ZERO`, zero-churn — every construction uses `..Default::default()`) + `SimulationRequest::block_ref()` (the real resolver forks by hash anyway). **Retract incidents (§15):** pure **`plan_retractions(&BlockReverted, &[IncidentId]) → Vec<IncidentRetracted>`** (`assert_eq!`-testable like [result.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/result.rs), reason names the orphaned + replacement block) driven by the **`ReorgConsumer`** shell (mirrors the `Dispatcher`: split decision-core/Kafka-loop, own consumer group, at-least-once — commits the offset only after publishing, and retraction is idempotent since the projection dedups a duplicate `IncidentRetracted`; a transient join fault **abandons** the offset to retry rather than silently skipping a reorg). `IncidentRetracted` re-enters keyed by incident id via t2's `result_partition_key` seam (free). Wired into the dispatcher binary alongside the alert dispatcher (`select!` on either loop exiting → cancel the peer → drain both). **The one acknowledged seam** (same honesty as the S5t3 `UnresolvedJobResolver` stub / detection's header-only no-op): the block→incident join **`IncidentIndex`** ships a documented stub **`EmptyIncidentIndex`** (→ retract nothing) — the incident schema carries no block (t2 locked "no schema change") so the join needs the by-block event-store read path that doesn't exist yet (the same query the resolver waits on). The plumbing is end-to-end (consume → plan → publish, all a no-op today); the live join lands with that query. **Tests:** +13 (`OrphanedBlocks` hash-not-height / idempotent-redelivery / FIFO-bounded; `SharedOrphanedBlocks` + `NeverOrphaned`; `plan_retractions` one-per-incident-shared-reason / empty; `ReorgConsumer` publishes-per-incident-and-commits / no-incidents-no-op / transient-fault-abandons; `revert_from_envelope` + topic; worker `a_job_for_an_orphaned_block_is_cancelled_without_simulating`) — **71 lib tests green**. clippy `-D warnings` (+ `--all-features`) + fmt clean; both binaries build; workspace green. |
| 5 | Postgres for in-flight jobs + confirmed incidents; ClickHouse incident analytics projection (§14) | 6h | DONE — the pure [`IncidentProjection`](../../crates/simulation/src/projection.rs) fold (t3) now has a store **behind** it, exactly as its docs promised. A new **[`simulation-projection`](../../crates/simulation/src/bin/projection.rs)** binary (third `[[bin]]`, so persistence scales independently of the dispatcher + revm worker — it's a Kafka projection, not a revm worker) consumes the five result-path topics via the shared [`event_bus::run_consumer`](../../crates/event-bus/src/lib.rs) loop, folds each event through the idempotent projection, and **write-throughs** to the two stores §14 assigns simulation. **Decisions (with the user):** persistence lives in the standalone consumer (not the worker's hot path); the in-flight **jobs table is derived from the event stream** (`SimulationRequested`→in-flight, `SimulationCompleted`→done), so dispatcher/worker are untouched. **[store.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/store.rs):** two object-safe seams — [`IncidentStore`](../../crates/simulation/src/store.rs) (Postgres/sqlx: the mutable `sim_jobs` + confirmed-`incidents` read model, full-row `ON CONFLICT` upserts; the `sim_jobs` upsert is independently **monotonic** in SQL — a `completed` job can't regress to `requested`, first-seen timestamps kept via `COALESCE`, since jobs aren't folded) and [`IncidentAnalytics`](../../crates/simulation/src/store.rs) (ClickHouse RowBinary append — the append-only `incident_analytics` firehose for by-kind/severity/window scans). Row mappings are pure/`assert_eq!`-testable; `AlertKind`/`Severity`/`IncidentStatus`→string via **exhaustive** matches (a new variant is a compile error, with a test asserting they match the serde wire form). **Idempotency under redelivery (the crux):** the incident row is upserted on **both `Updated` and `Duplicate`** (idempotent, and it closes the gap where a store fault → `Retry` → redelivery re-folds to `Duplicate` and would otherwise drop the never-persisted row — the queryable read model stays exact); the analytics append is gated on `Updated` only (dedup-consistent — a crash-rerun's identical figures under a fresh event id fold to `Duplicate`, so no double-count). At-least-once: the offset commits only after write-through, a store fault leaves it for redelivery. **Migrations:** Postgres pair in [crates/db/migrations](../../crates/db/migrations) applied by sqlx-cli (`just migrate-*`), the shared pool builder in the [`db`](../../crates/db/src/lib.rs) crate; ClickHouse via a runner ported from the event store ([ch_migrate.rs](../../crates/simulation/src/ch_migrate.rs), own `sim_schema_migrations`) run at boot + a `simulation-projection migrate {up,down,info}` subcommand (`just sim-ch-migrate-*`). sqlx queries are compile-time-checked with the committed [`.sqlx`](../../.sqlx) offline cache (CI `SQLX_OFFLINE=true`); `sqlx`/`tls-rustls` pinned to the installed cli version, `cargo deny` clean. **Known seam (documented):** on restart the in-memory fold starts empty (offsets committed), so a terminal event for a pre-restart `IncidentCreated` orphan-buffers — follow-up is rehydrating the `incident_id→alert_id` links from Postgres at boot (same honesty as the `UnresolvedJobResolver`/`EmptyIncidentIndex` stubs). **Tests:** +9 unit (row mappings; job derivation; consumer write-through fires on `Updated`, re-upserts on `Duplicate` without double-appending analytics, retries on store fault, orphaned-terminal persists nothing until linked) + a live-store **integration suite** ([tests/projection_store.rs](../../crates/simulation/tests/projection_store.rs), `#[ignore]`, testcontainers Postgres + ClickHouse): incident upsert idempotent + `sim_jobs` status monotonic; analytics append + `GROUP BY kind` aggregation. **93 lib tests green** (84 sim + doc/others), clippy `-D warnings` (+ `--all-features`) + fmt clean, all three binaries build (release), live-verified against the running Postgres + ClickHouse (both tables created via the recipes). |

**Deliverable:** End-to-end `BlockAssembled → PreliminaryAlert → SimulationJob → IncidentCreated` is auditable in the event store. **Phase 3 complete.**
